    readonly method: "post";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/me/email/cancel";
    readonly operationId: "cancelEmailChange";
    readonly tag: "me";
    readonly summary: "Cancel an email change and sign out everywhere with the \"this wasn't me\" link";
    readonly auth: "anonymous";
    readonly rate: "token_redeem";
    readonly request: {
        readonly body: z.ZodObject<{
            token: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 204: EmptyResponse<Readonly<Record<string, ResponseHeader>>>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 403: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 410: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export const CancelEmailChangeRequestSchema: z.ZodObject<{
    token: z.ZodString;
}, z.core.$strict>;

// @public (undocumented)
export const cancelPendingEmailChange: {
    readonly method: "delete";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/me/email/pending";
    readonly operationId: "cancelPendingEmailChange";
    readonly tag: "me";
    readonly summary: "Cancel the account's pending email change";
    readonly auth: "account";
    readonly rate: "write";
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 204: EmptyResponse<Readonly<Record<string, ResponseHeader>>>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 401: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 403: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export const changePassword: {
    readonly method: "post";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/me/password";
    readonly operationId: "changePassword";
    readonly tag: "me";
    readonly summary: "Change the account's password, or set the first one";
    readonly auth: "account";
    readonly rate: "write";
    readonly reauth: true;
    readonly request: {
        readonly body: z.ZodObject<{
            currentPassword: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            newPassword: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 204: EmptyResponse<Readonly<Record<string, ResponseHeader>>>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 401: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 403: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export const ChangePasswordRequestSchema: z.ZodObject<{
    currentPassword: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    newPassword: z.ZodString;
}, z.core.$strict>;

// @public (undocumented)
export const checkSlugAvailability: {
    readonly method: "get";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/slugs/availability";
    readonly operationId: "checkSlugAvailability";
    readonly tag: "bookmarks";
    readonly summary: "Check whether a slug is free";
    readonly auth: "member";
    readonly rate: "read";
    readonly scope: "read";
    readonly request: {
        readonly query: z.ZodObject<{
            slug: z.ZodString;
            bookmarkId: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodUUID>;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 200: JsonResponse<z.ZodObject<{
        status: z.ZodEnum<{
        available: "available";
        taken_by_me: "taken_by_me";
        invalid: "invalid";
        }>;
        othersUseCount: z.ZodNumber;
        }, z.core.$strict>, Readonly<Record<string, ResponseHeader>>>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 401: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 404: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export const chooseSlug: {
    readonly method: "post";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/go/choose";
    readonly operationId: "chooseSlug";
    readonly tag: "go";
    readonly summary: "Choose a bookmark of a slug and get its destination";
    readonly auth: "member";
    readonly rate: "go";
    readonly scope: "write";
    readonly request: {
        readonly body: z.ZodObject<{
            slug: z.ZodString;
            bookmarkId: z.ZodUUID;
            remember: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodBoolean>;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 200: JsonResponse<z.ZodObject<{
        url: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>, Readonly<Record<string, ResponseHeader>>>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 401: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 403: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 404: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export const completeOidcSignIn: {
    readonly method: "get";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/auth/oidc/{provider}/callback";
    readonly operationId: "completeOidcSignIn";
    readonly tag: "auth";
    readonly summary: "Finish signing in with an OIDC provider";
    readonly auth: "anonymous";
    readonly rate: "login";
    readonly request: {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            provider: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 302: EmptyResponse<    {
        Location: {
        description: string;
        schema: z.ZodString;
        };
        "Set-Cookie": {
        description: string;
        schema: z.ZodString;
        };
        }>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export const completeSetup: {
    readonly method: "post";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/setup";
    readonly operationId: "completeSetup";
    readonly tag: "setup";
    readonly summary: "Create the first account, instance admin and owner of the first workspace, and sign it in";
    readonly auth: "anonymous";
    readonly rate: "setup";
    readonly request: {
        readonly body: z.ZodObject<{
            setupToken: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            displayName: z.ZodString;
            email: z.ZodEmail;
            password: z.ZodString;
            workspaceName: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 201: JsonResponse<z.ZodObject<{
        workspaceId: z.ZodUUID;
        }, z.core.$strict>, {
        "Set-Cookie": {
        description: string;
        schema: z.ZodString;
        };
        }>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 403: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

// @public (undocumented)
export interface ComponentTypes {
    // (undocumented)
    headers: never;
    // (undocumented)
    parameters: never;
    // (undocumented)
    pathItems: never;
    // (undocumented)
    requestBodies: never;
    // (undocumented)
    responses: never;
    // (undocumented)
    schemas: {
        AcceptInvitationRequest: {
            displayName?: string;
            password?: string;
            token: string;
        };
        Account: {
            accent: ("periwinkle" | "violet" | "rose" | "amber" | "green" | "teal") | null;
            aiOptOut: boolean;
            analyticsConsent: "unset" | "granted" | "denied";
            defaultView: "grid" | "table";
            displayName: string;
            email: string;
            emailVerified: boolean;
            hasPassword: boolean;
            id: string;
            isInstanceAdmin: boolean;
            locale: "en" | "de";
            mfaEnabled: boolean;
            onboarding: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Onboarding"];
            pendingEmail: string | null;
            signinAlerts: boolean;
            singleKeyShortcuts: boolean;
            theme: "light" | "dark" | "system";
        };
        ApiConfig: {
            apiVersion: "v1";
            defaultLocale: string;
            docsBaseUrl: string;
            features: {
                ai: boolean;
                mail: boolean;
            };
            instance?: {
                displayName: string;
                signInNotice: string | null;
            };
            locales: string[];
            origin: string;
            product: {
                name: string;
                version: string;
            };
            setupRequired?: boolean;
            setupTokenRequired?: boolean;
            signIn?: {
                oidc: {
                    name: string;
                    slug: string;
                }[];
                registration: boolean;
            };
        };
        ApiToken: {
            createdAt: string;
            expiresAt: string | null;
            id: string;
            lastUsedAt: string | null;
            name: string;
            prefix: string;
            scope: "read" | "read-write";
            workspaceId: string;
            workspaceName: string;
        };
        ApiTokenList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["ApiToken"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
        };
        Bookmark: {
            createdAt: string;
            description: string | null;
            folders: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["BookmarkFolder"][];
            forwarding: boolean;
            host: string;
            id: string;
            lastOpenedAt: string | null;
            openCount: number;
            ownerId: string | null;
            pinned: boolean;
            slug: string | null;
            tags: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["BookmarkTag"][];
            title: string;
            updatedAt: string;
            url: string;
            version: number;
        };
        BookmarkFilter: {
            folderId?: string;
            forwarding?: boolean;
            hasSlug?: boolean;
            pinned?: boolean;
            q?: string;
            scope?: "all" | "mine";
            tag?: string | string[];
            url?: string;
        };
        BookmarkFolder: {
            color: number;
            icon: string;
            id: string;
            name: string;
        };
        BookmarkIds: {
            ids: string[];
            truncated: boolean;
        };
        BookmarkList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Bookmark"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
            total: number | null;
            totalAtLeast?: 10000;
        };
        BookmarkMetadata: {
            cached: boolean;
            canonicalUrl: string | null;
            description: string | null;
            language: string | null;
            siteName: string | null;
            title: string | null;
        };
        BookmarkMetadataRequest: {
            url: string;
        };
        BookmarkOpen: {
            url: string;
        };
        BookmarkShare: {
            accountId: string | null;
            createdAt: string;
            email: string | null;
            id: string;
            kind: "member" | "team";
            name: string;
            teamId: string | null;
        };
        BookmarkShareList: {
            audienceSize: number;
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["BookmarkShare"][];
        };
        BookmarkTag: {
            id: string;
            name: string;
        };
        BulkBookmarksPreview: {
            affected: number;
            requested: number;
            skipped: number;
            skippedReasons: {
                not_found: number;
                not_owner: number;
            };
            tags: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["BulkPreviewTag"][];
        };
        BulkBookmarksPreviewRequest: {
            action: "add_tags";
            filter?: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["BookmarkFilter"];
            ids?: string[];
            tagIds: string[];
        };
        BulkBookmarksRequest: {
            action: "delete" | "pin" | "unpin" | "add_to_folder" | "move_to_folder" | "remove_from_folder" | "add_tags" | "remove_tags";
            filter?: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["BookmarkFilter"];
            folderId?: string;
            ids?: string[];
            tagIds?: string[];
        };
        BulkBookmarksResult: {
            affected: number;
            requested: number;
            skipped: number;
            skippedReasons: {
                not_found: number;
                not_owner: number;
            };
        };
        BulkPreviewTag: {
            added: number;
            bookmarkCount: number;
            id: string;
            name: string;
        };
        CancelEmailChangeRequest: {
            token: string;
        };
        ChangePasswordRequest: {
            currentPassword?: string;
            newPassword: string;
        };
        ConfirmEmailChangeRequest: {
            token: string;
        };
        ConfirmMfaEnrollmentRequest: {
            code: string;
        };
        CreateApiTokenRequest: {
            expiresInDays: (30 | 90 | 365) | null;
            name: string;
            scope: "read" | "read-write";
            workspaceId?: string;
        };
        CreateBookmarkRequest: {
            description?: string;
            folderIds?: string[];
            forwarding?: boolean;
            pinned?: boolean;
            slug?: string;
            tagIds?: string[];
            tagNames?: string[];
            title: string;
            url: string;
        };
        CreateBookmarkShareRequest: {
            accountId: string;
        } | {
            teamId: string;
        };
        CreateFolderRequest: {
            color?: number;
            icon?: "folder" | "folder-open" | "folder-heart" | "folder-code" | "folder-archive" | "briefcase" | "building-2" | "book" | "book-open" | "bookmark" | "star" | "heart" | "house" | "newspaper" | "graduation-cap" | "flask-conical" | "code" | "terminal" | "cpu" | "database" | "server" | "cloud" | "globe" | "map" | "map-pin" | "plane" | "car" | "utensils" | "coffee" | "shopping-cart" | "gift" | "music" | "film" | "camera" | "image" | "video" | "gamepad-2" | "palette" | "lightbulb" | "rocket" | "target" | "trophy" | "flag" | "tag" | "wallet" | "credit-card" | "dumbbell" | "leaf" | "sun" | "moon" | "zap" | "shield" | "lock" | "users" | "mail" | "calendar" | "inbox" | "package" | "wrench" | "sparkles";
            name: string;
        };
        CreateInstanceWorkspaceRequest: {
            name: string;
            ownerAccountId?: string;
            ownerEmail?: string;
        };
        CreateInvitationRequest: {
            email: string;
            role: "admin" | "member";
            teamIds?: string[];
        };
        CreateTagRequest: {
            name: string;
        };
        CreateTeamRequest: {
            description?: string;
            name: string;
        };
        CreateWorkspaceRequest: {
            name: string;
        };
        CreatedApiToken: {
            createdAt: string;
            expiresAt: string | null;
            id: string;
            lastUsedAt: string | null;
            name: string;
            prefix: string;
            scope: "read" | "read-write";
            token: string;
            workspaceId: string;
            workspaceName: string;
        };
        Dashboard: {
            counts: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["DashboardCounts"];
            onboarding: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["DashboardOnboarding"];
            pinned: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["DashboardBookmark"][];
            quickAccess: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["DashboardBookmark"][];
            sharing: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["DashboardSharing"] | null;
            topTags: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["DashboardTag"][];
        };
        DashboardBookmark: {
            forwarding: boolean;
            host: string;
            id: string;
            openCount: number;
            slug: string | null;
            title: string;
            url: string;
        };
        DashboardCounts: {
            bookmarks: number;
            folders: number;
            sharedWithYou: number | null;
            tags: number;
        };
        DashboardOnboarding: {
            bookmarkAdded: boolean;
            browserSetupDone: boolean;
            dismissed: boolean;
            folderCreated: boolean;
            importDone: boolean;
            slugGiven: boolean;
        };
        DashboardSharing: {
            sharedByYou: number;
            sharedWithYou: number;
        };
        DashboardTag: {
            bookmarkCount: number;
            id: string;
            name: string;
        };
        DeleteAccountRequest: {
            email: string;
        };
        DisableMfaRequest: {
            code: string;
            password?: string;
        } | {
            backupCode: string;
            password?: string;
        };
        EndMembershipRequest: {
            content: "transfer" | "delete";
            transferTo?: string;
        };
        EntitlementSet: {
            "ai.suggestions": boolean;
            "audit.log": boolean;
            "bookmarks.max": number | "unlimited";
            "members.invite": boolean;
            "seats.max": number | "unlimited";
            "sharing.members": boolean;
            "sharing.teams": boolean;
            "teams.manage": boolean;
            "workspaces.ownMax": number | "unlimited";
        };
        EntitlementUsage: {
            "bookmarks.used": number;
            "seats.used": number;
            "workspaces.owned": number;
        };
        Entitlements: {
            entitlements: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["EntitlementSet"];
            upgradeAvailable: boolean;
            usage: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["EntitlementUsage"];
        };
        Folder: {
            bookmarkCount: number;
            color: number;
            createdAt: string;
            icon: string;
            id: string;
            name: string;
            ownerId: string | null;
            updatedAt: string;
            version: number;
        };
        FolderBookmarksRequest: {
            bookmarkIds: string[];
        };
        FolderList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Folder"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
            total: number | null;
            totalAtLeast?: 10000;
        };
        ForgotPasswordRequest: {
            email: string;
        };
        GoCandidate: {
            host: string;
            id: string;
            owner: {
                accountId: string;
                displayName: string;
            } | null;
            sharedVia: ("direct" | "team" | "folder") | null;
            title: string;
        };
        GoDestination: {
            url: string;
        };
        GoPreference: {
            bookmarkId: string;
            host: string;
            owner: {
                accountId: string;
                displayName: string;
            } | null;
            slug: string;
            title: string;
        };
        GoPreferenceList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["GoPreference"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
        };
        GoResolution: {
            otherWorkspaces: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["GoWorkspace"][];
            result: "none";
        } | {
            bookmark: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["GoCandidate"];
            result: "preferred";
        } | {
            bookmark: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["GoCandidate"];
            result: "single";
        } | {
            candidates: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["GoCandidate"][];
            result: "multiple";
        };
        GoSuggestion: {
            bookmarkId: string;
            host: string;
            owner: {
                accountId: string;
                displayName: string;
            } | null;
            sharedVia: ("direct" | "team" | "folder") | null;
            slug: string;
            title: string;
        };
        GoSuggestions: {
            suggestions: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["GoSuggestion"][];
        };
        GoWorkspace: {
            id: string;
            name: string;
        };
        HealthStatus: {
            status: "ok";
        };
        Identity: {
            createdAt: string;
            emailAtLink: string;
            id: string;
            lastUsedAt: string | null;
            provider: string;
        };
        IdentityList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Identity"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
        };
        InspectInvitationRequest: {
            token: string;
        };
        InstanceAccount: {
            disabled: boolean;
            displayName: string;
            email: string;
            emailVerified: boolean;
            id: string;
            isInstanceAdmin: boolean;
            lastSignInAt: string | null;
            mfaEnabled: boolean;
            workspaceCount: number;
        };
        InstanceAccountList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["InstanceAccount"][];
        };
        InstanceAccountResetLink: {
            delivery: "mail" | "link";
            link: string | null;
        };
        InstanceOwnerInvitation: {
            email: string;
            expiresAt: string;
            id: string;
            link: string | null;
        };
        InstanceSettings: {
            allowWorkspaceCreation: boolean | null;
            displayName: string | null;
            signInNotice: string | null;
        };
        InstanceStatus: {
            counts: {
                accounts: number;
                activeSessions: number;
                workspaces: number;
            };
            jobs: {
                failed: number;
                queueDepth: number;
            };
            migrations: {
                applied: number;
                expected: number;
            };
            oidcProviders: {
                name: string;
                slug: string;
            }[];
            ports: {
                ai: boolean;
                errorReporting: boolean;
                mail: boolean;
            };
            signIn: {
                emailVerificationRequired: boolean;
                publicRegistration: boolean;
            };
            version: string;
        };
        InstanceWorkspace: {
            createdAt: string;
            id: string;
            memberCount: number;
            name: string;
            owners: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["InstanceWorkspaceOwner"][];
        };
        InstanceWorkspaceCreated: {
            id: string;
            name: string;
            ownerInvitation: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["InstanceOwnerInvitation"] | null;
        };
        InstanceWorkspaceList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["InstanceWorkspace"][];
        };
        InstanceWorkspaceMembership: {
            role: "member";
            workspaceId: string;
        };
        InstanceWorkspaceOwner: {
            accountId: string;
            displayName: string;
            email: string;
        };
        Invitation: {
            createdAt: string;
            email: string;
            expiresAt: string;
            id: string;
            invitedBy: string | null;
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
            status: "pending" | "accepted" | "revoked";
            teamIds: string[];
        };
        InvitationAccepted: {
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
            workspaceId: string;
        };
        InvitationLink: {
            invitation: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Invitation"];
            link: string;
        };
        InvitationList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["InvitationListItem"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
        };
        InvitationListItem: {
            createdAt: string;
            email: string;
            expired: boolean;
            expiresAt: string;
            id: string;
            invitedBy: string | null;
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
            status: "pending" | "accepted" | "revoked";
            teamIds: string[];
        };
        InvitationPreview: {
            accountExists: boolean;
            email: string;
            inviterName: string | null;
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
            workspaceName: string;
        };
        IssueInstanceOwnerInvitationRequest: {
            ownerEmail?: string;
        };
        LoginRequest: {
            email: string;
            password: string;
            remember: boolean;
        };
        LoginResult: {
            next: "done" | "mfa";
        };
        Me: {
            account: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Account"];
            activeWorkspaceId: string | null;
            clientErrorReportDsn?: string;
            memberships: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Membership"][];
        };
        Member: {
            accountId: string;
            displayName: string;
            email: string;
            joinedAt: string;
            lastActiveAt: string | null;
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
        };
        MemberList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Member"][];
            nextCursor: string | null;
        };
        Membership: {
            name: string;
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
            workspaceId: string;
        };
        MembershipEnded: {
            content: "transfer" | "delete";
            transferTo: string;
        };
        MergeTagRequest: {
            intoTagId: string;
        };
        MfaBackupCodes: {
            backupCodes: string[];
        };
        MfaEnrollment: {
            otpauthUri: string;
            qrSvg: string;
            secret: string;
        };
        Onboarding: {
            browserSetupDone: boolean;
            checklistDismissed: boolean;
            importDone: boolean;
        };
        OnboardingChanges: {
            browserSetupDone?: boolean;
            checklistDismissed?: boolean;
            importDone?: boolean;
        };
        OpenApiDocument: {
            info: {
                [key: string]: unknown;
            };
            openapi: string;
            paths: {
                [key: string]: unknown;
            };
        } & {
            [key: string]: unknown;
        };
        Problem: {
            code: "bad_request" | "unauthenticated" | "mfa_required" | "reauth_required" | "forbidden" | "registration_refused" | "cross_site_request" | "token_scope" | "entitlement_required" | "email_unverified" | "registration_closed" | "setup_completed" | "not_found" | "slug_not_found" | "slug_taken" | "name_taken" | "email_taken" | "already_member" | "last_owner" | "workspace_has_owner" | "idempotency_conflict" | "billing_active" | "token_expired" | "precondition_failed" | "payload_too_large" | "unsupported_media_type" | "validation_failed" | "bookmark_limit_reached" | "seat_limit_reached" | "workspace_limit_reached" | "weak_password" | "invalid_code" | "url_not_allowed" | "rate_limited" | "internal" | "upstream_failed" | "unavailable" | "mail_unavailable" | "ai_unavailable" | "billing_unavailable";
            detail: string;
            errors?: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["ProblemFieldError"][];
            requestId: string;
            status: number;
            title: string;
            type: string;
        } & {
            [key: string]: unknown;
        };
        ProblemFieldError: {
            code: string;
            path: string;
        };
        ReadyStatus: {
            database: "ok" | "unreachable";
            migrations: {
                applied: number | null;
                expected: number;
            };
        };
        ReauthRequest: {
            password: string;

        };

        };

        };
        SetupResult: {

        };

            setupTokenRequired: boolean;
        };
        ShareTarget: {
            email: string | null;
            id: string;
            kind: "member" | "team";
            name: string;
        };
        ShareTargetList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["ShareTarget"][];
        };
        SlugAvailability: {

            status: "available" | "taken_by_me" | "invalid";
        };
        Tag: {

            createdAt: string;
            id: string;
            name: string;
        };
        TagList: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Tag"][];

            total: number | null;

        };
        TagStats: {
            items: ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Tag"][];

            totalAtLeast?: 10000;
        };
        Team: {

            description: string;

            updatedAt: string;
            version: number;
        };
        TeamList: {

            memberCount: number;

        UpdateAccountRequest: {

            pinned?: boolean;

            tagIds?: string[];

            icon?: "folder" | "folder-open" | "folder-heart" | "folder-code" | "folder-archive" | "briefcase" | "building-2" | "book" | "book-open" | "bookmark" | "star" | "heart" | "house" | "newspaper" | "graduation-cap" | "flask-conical" | "code" | "terminal" | "cpu" | "database" | "server" | "cloud" | "globe" | "map" | "map-pin" | "plane" | "car" | "utensils" | "coffee" | "shopping-cart" | "gift" | "music" | "film" | "camera" | "image" | "video" | "gamepad-2" | "palette" | "lightbulb" | "rocket" | "target" | "trophy" | "flag" | "tag" | "wallet" | "credit-card" | "dumbbell" | "leaf" | "sun" | "moon" | "zap" | "shield" | "lock" | "users" | "mail" | "calendar" | "inbox" | "package" | "wrench" | "sparkles";
            name?: string;
        };
        UpdateInstanceAccountRequest: {
            disabled?: boolean;
            emailVerified?: true;
            isInstanceAdmin?: boolean;
        };
        UpdateInstanceSettingsRequest: {
            allowWorkspaceCreation?: boolean | null;
            displayName?: string | null;
            signInNotice?: string | null;
        };
        UpdateMemberRequest: {
            role: "owner" | "admin" | "member";
        };
        UpdateTeamRequest: {
            description?: string;
            name?: string;
        };
        UpdateWorkspaceSettingsRequest: {
            aiEnabled?: boolean;
        };
        VerifyEmailRequest: {
            password: string;
            token: string;
        };
        VerifyEmailResult: {
            next: "done" | "mfa";
        };
        VerifyMfaRequest: {

        } | {

        };

        VersionInfo: {

            name: string;
            version: string;
        };
        Workspace: {

            id: string;
            memberCount: number;
            name: string;

        };

}

export interface OperationDefinition {
    // (undocumented)
    readonly audience?: Audience;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly auth: AuthPolicy;

    readonly entitlement?: string;
    readonly idempotency?: boolean;
    readonly maxBodyBytes?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly method: HttpMethod;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly operationId: string;
    readonly path: string;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly rate: RateBucket;
    readonly rateTakenByHandler?: boolean;
    readonly reauth?: boolean;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly request?: OperationRequest;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly responses: OperationResponses;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly scope?: TokenScope;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly summary?: string;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly tag: string;
}

export interface OperationRequest {
    readonly body?: z.ZodType;

    readonly query?: z.ZodObject;
}

    [S in OtherResponseStatus]?: JsonResponse | EmptyResponse | ProblemResponse | BinaryResponse;

    [S in ProblemResponseStatus]: S extends 429 ? RateLimitedResponse : ProblemResponse;
}>;

                headers: {
                    [name: string]: unknown;
                };
                content: {
                    "application/problem+json": ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Problem"];
                };
            };
            429: {
                headers: {
                    "Retry-After": number;
                    [name: string]: unknown;
                };
                content: {
                    "application/problem+json": ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Problem"];
                };
            };
            503: {
                headers: {
                    [name: string]: unknown;
                };
                content: {
                    "application/problem+json": ComponentTypes["schemas"]["Problem"];
                };
            };
        };
    };
}

// @public (undocumented)
export const previewBulkBookmarks: {
    readonly method: "post";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/bookmarks/bulk/preview";
    readonly operationId: "previewBulkBookmarks";
    readonly tag: "bookmarks";
    readonly summary: "Preview adding tags to many bookmarks";
    readonly auth: "member";
    readonly rate: "read";
    readonly scope: "write";
    readonly request: {
        readonly body: z.ZodObject<{
            action: z.ZodLiteral<"add_tags">;
            ids: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodArray<z.ZodUUID>>;
            filter: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{
                scope: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{
                    all: "all";
                    mine: "mine";
                }>>;
                pinned: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodBoolean>;
                hasSlug: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodBoolean>;
                forwarding: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodBoolean>;
                q: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                url: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                folderId: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodUUID>;
                tag: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodUnion<readonly [z.ZodUUID, z.ZodArray<z.ZodUUID>]>>;
            }, z.core.$strict>>;
            tagIds: z.ZodArray<z.ZodUUID>;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 200: JsonResponse<z.ZodObject<{
        requested: z.ZodNumber;
        affected: z.ZodNumber;
        skipped: z.ZodNumber;
        skippedReasons: z.ZodObject<{
        not_owner: z.ZodNumber;
        not_found: z.ZodNumber;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        tags: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodUUID;
        name: z.ZodString;
        bookmarkCount: z.ZodNumber;
        added: z.ZodNumber;
        }, z.core.$strict>>;
        }, z.core.$strict>, Readonly<Record<string, ResponseHeader>>>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 401: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 403: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 404: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};

export const RATE_BUCKETS: readonly ["login", "mfa", "register", "reset", "token_redeem", "token_create", "setup", "go", "read", "write", "bulk", "fetch", "ai", "public", "none"];

}

export const register: {
    readonly method: "post";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/auth/register";
    readonly operationId: "register";

    readonly summary: "Register an account when public registration is open";
    readonly auth: "anonymous";
    readonly rate: "register";
    readonly request: {

export const startOidcSignIn: {
    readonly method: "get";
    readonly path: "/api/v1/auth/oidc/{provider}/start";
    readonly operationId: "startOidcSignIn";
    readonly tag: "auth";
    readonly summary: "Start signing in with an OIDC provider";
    readonly auth: "anonymous";
    readonly rate: "login";
    readonly request: {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            provider: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly query: z.ZodObject<{
            returnTo: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            intent: z.ZodDefault<z.ZodEnum<{
                login: "login";
                link: "link";
                reauth: "reauth";
                invite: "invite";
            }>>;
            invitation: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
    };
    readonly responses: {
        readonly 302: EmptyResponse<    {
        readonly Location: {
        readonly description: "Where the browser goes next: the provider's authorization endpoint, or /login?error=oidc_failed when the handshake cannot start";
        readonly schema: z.ZodString;
        };
        readonly "Set-Cookie": {
        readonly description: "The pre-auth cookie that binds the handshake to this browser: HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Path=/, valid for 10 minutes, Secure and __Host- prefixed when the deployment is served over https; cleared when no handshake started";
        readonly schema: z.ZodString;
        };
        }>;
        readonly 400: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 401: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 422: ProblemResponse;
        readonly 429: RateLimitedResponse;
        readonly 503: ProblemResponse;
    };
};
